- **slack-formatting**: Formatting rules for composing Slack messages so they render correctly (mrkdwn, not Markdown). Trigger on "post this to Slack", "format this for Slack", "send a Slack message", "reply in the thread", or before any slack_chat_post_message call. Covers mrkdwn vs Markdown, code blocks, link `&lt;url|label&gt;` and mention `&lt;@Uxxx&gt;`/`&lt;#Cxxx&gt;` syntax, no-Markdown-tables, and thread-vs-channel etiquette.
